We can also use "surrogate linearization": at current a, F(a+δ)≈F+⟨g,δ⟩. The projected step chooses decrease in linear model. Since model may be optimistic; accept if F decreases. Use t from previous success.

Projected simplex implementation:
```
def project_simplex(v):
    # v np array
    # sort descending
    u=np.sort(v)[::-1]
    cssum=np.cumsum(u)
    rho=np.arange(1, v.size+1)
    cond=u - (cssum - 1)/rho
    mask=cond>0
    theta=(cssum[mask][-1] - 1)/rho[mask][-1]
    return np.maximum(v - theta,0)
```
For n=4806, sort each projection O(n log n) ~ maybe 10µs? Actually sort 5k ~0.1ms, fine. 100k projections=10s maybe okay. Could use linear-time but not necessary.

    a=softmax(y)

    return obj, g

But L-BFGS-B's line search may evaluate many times. The gradient computation must be fast. We can precompute N. Concern: L-BFGS-B on nonconvex with τ schedule may not improve enough in limited iterations. Projected gradient many simple iterations may be better.

Let's design projected gradient algorithm carefully to make monotone-ish improvements.

Now simplex projection. For performance, can use sorting. `v_theta`: v can be negative large. We need project onto sum=1. Algorithm if all v<... Actually v sum not necessarily. Implementation:
```
def project_simplex(v):
    u = np.sort(v)[::-1]
    cssum = np.cumsum(u)
    rho = np.arange(1, v.size+1)
    cond = u > (cssum - 1.0) / rho
    if not np.any(cond):
       # fallback? theta?
    theta = (cssum[np.nonzero(cond)[0][-1]] - 1.0) / rho[...]
    out = np.maximum(v - theta, 0.0)
    # ensure sum exactly 1
    s=out.sum()
    if s>0: out/=s
    else out=np.ones_like(v)/v.size
    return out
```
Since we project many times, using `np.nonzero(cond)[0][-1]` may be okay. Better use `cond_sum = np.cumsum(cond); idx = cond_sum[-1]`. Actually rho index = cond_sum[-1] (number of cond true), theta=(cssum[idx-1]-1)/idx. Use last_true_idx = cond_sum[-1]; theta=(cssum[last_true_idx-1]-1)/last_true_idx.

If shift constant, F_shift = τ log(Σ exp(b/τ)) exactly up to constant? Using M_cur shift gives value τ(log Σ e^{(b-M_cur)/τ}) = Fτ - M_cur? Actually Fτ=τ logΣe^{b/τ}= M_cur + τ logΣe^{(b-M_cur)/τ}. Pseudo iteration:
```
for iter in range(max_iter):
  b = conv(a)
  M = b.max()
  tau = max(tau_frac*M, 1e-12)
  w = softmax((b-M)/tau)
  Fcur = M + tau*log(w? actually log_sum)
  grad = grad(w)
  success=False
  try_t = t
  best_candidate=None; best_F=Fcur
  for attempt in range(12):
     P = project(a - try_t*grad)
     if not finite or sum==0: break
     bP=conv(P); MP=bP.max()

Objective:
```
def fun_grad(y):
   y = np.clip(y, -50, 50)
   a = softmax(y) # stable
   b = conv(a)
   M = b.max(); tau = tau_lbfgs*M
   sh=(b-M)/tau; sh[sh>700]=700
   sumexp = np.exp(sh).sum()
   F = 2*n*(M + tau*math.log(sumexp))
   w = np.exp(sh)/sumexp
   grad_a = 2*extract
   gsum = grad_a.sum()
   gy = grad_a - a*gsum
   return F, gy
```

Block average function:
```
def block_expand(x, B):
   n=len(x); a=np.zeros(n)
   for b in range(B):
      l=b*n//B; r=(b+1)*n//B
      mean=np.mean(x[l:r]) if r>l else 0
      a[l:r]=mean
   s=a.sum(); return a/s if s>0 else x
```

Let's refine `_optimize` code for time and robustness.

```
def _optimize(a0, deadline, rng):
    a = _normalize(a0)
    best_a=a.copy(); best_score=eval(a)
    N=...; L=2*n-1
    tau_frac=0.08
    t=1.0
    stall=0; exact_stall=0; kick_count=0
    iter=0
    best_approx=best_score/(2*n)
    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 5:
       iter+=1
       # manage tau
       b=conv_b(a)
       M=b.max()
       if M <=0: break
       Fcur_exact? no
       tau=max(tau_frac*M, 1e-12)
       sh=(b-M)/tau; clip; exp; sumexp; Fcur=M+tau*log(sumexp)
       w=exp/sumexp
       # grad
       ...
       # line search
       # maybe norm_grad; if norm too small break
       normg=np.linalg.norm(grad)
       if normg < 1e-15: break
       success=False; best_candidate=None; best_F=Fcur
       tt=t
       for _ in range(8):
           P = project_simplex(a - tt*grad)
           # maybe keep values >=0
           bP=conv_b(P); MP=bP.max()
           sp=(bP-M)/tau; clip; sumexpP; Fp=M+tau*log(sumexpP)
           if Fp < best_F:
               best_F=Fp; best_candidate=P
           if Fp <= Fcur - 1e-12*(1+abs(Fcur)) or (MP + 0 < M - 1e-12):
               a=P; t=min(tt*1.05, 200.0); stall=0; success=True; break
           tt*=0.5
       if not success:
           if best_candidate is not None and best_F <= Fcur + 1e-12:
               a=best_candidate; t *=0.7
           else:
               t=max(t*0.5, 1e-3)
           stall+=1
       # update exact occasionally
       if iter%100 == 0 or (2*n*M) < best_score*0.999:
           sc=eval(a)
           if sc<best_score:
                best_score=sc; best_a=a.copy(); exact_stall=0; best_approx=sc/(2*n)
           else: exact_stall += 100
       # if target? track
       # stalls
       if stall > 60:
           if tau_frac > 0.02:
               tau_frac *= 0.8
           else:
               t = min(t*2, 50)
           stall=0
       if exact_stall > 400 and kick_count < 6 and time...:
           # kick
           v = a + rng.normal(0, 0.2/n, n)
           a = project_simplex(np.maximum(v,0))
           t=1.0; tau_frac=0.08; stall=0; exact_stall=0; kick_count+=1
       if time... break
    final eval
    return best_a, best_score
```

if sh_max > 700: return sh_max + log(sum(exp(sh-sh_max)))

Better stable:
```
def logsumexp_s(x):
   m=x.max()
   if m < -745: return m  # approximation
   return m + math.log(np.exp(x-m).sum())
```

```
def _coord_polish(a0, deadline, rng):
    a=normalize(a0); best=eval(a); best_a=a.copy()
    N... L
    it=0
    while time<deadline-10:
       it+=1
       # choose random index; maybe bias to nonzero? Use rng.integers(n)
       i=rng.integers(n)
       old=a[i]
       b=conv_b(a)  # recompute each coordinate
       # build d once
       idx = np.arange(L) - i
       mask = (idx>=0)&(idx<n)&(idx!=0)
       d = np.zeros(L); d[mask]=2*a[idx[mask]]
       eidx = 2*i; eadd_idxs valid if eidx<L
       # candidates
       if old == 0:
           mean = a.mean()
           factors = [0.0, 0.1, 1.0, 5.0] # times mean? But coordinate could need large? 
           cand = [mean*f for f in [0.02,0.1,1,5]] maybe include old? old 0.
       else:
           cand = [old*f for f in (0.25,0.5,0.75,1.25,1.5,2.0)]
       cand.append(old) # ensure no change
       best_x=old; best_sc=eval? approximate current from b: current_sc = 2*n*b.max() (sum=1)
       for x in cand:
           if x<0: continue
           S=1+x-old
           if S <= 1e-12: continue
           # compute max efficiently
           # Instead of copying b each time, use tmp=b+d*(x-old)
           tmp = b + d*(x-old)
           if eidx < L: tmp[eidx] += x*x - old*old
           m=tmp.max()
           if m < 0: m=0
           sc = 2*n*m/(S*S)
           if sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
               best_sc=sc; best_x=x
       if best_x != old:
           a[i]=best_x
           s = a.sum()
           if s <=0: a=normalize_ones; continue
           a = a/s
           # exact evaluate periodically or if approximate improved
           # We can exact eval if sc < best - 1e-6 or it%50==0
           exact = eval(a) every 20 accepted or it%100
           if exact < best: best=exact; best_a=a.copy()
    return best_a,best
```

def _lbfgs_polish(a0, deadline):
    try:
      from scipy.optimize import minimize
    except: return a0, inf
    y0 = np.log(np.maximum(a0, 1e-300)); y0 -= y0.mean()
    tau_frac=0.03
    class Abort(Exception): pass
    def fgy(y):
       if time.monotonic() > deadline - 30: raise Abort
       y = np.clip(y, -50, 50)
       e=np.exp(y-y.max()); a=e/e.sum()
       b=conv_b(a)
       M=b.max(); tau=max(tau_frac*M,1e-12)
       sh=(b-M)/tau; shm=sh.max(); logs=...
       val = 2*n*(M+tau*logs)
       w=np.exp(sh - logsum?)? Need weights: exp(shifted - logs?) Since logs = logsumshift. `logs_shift = logsumexp_s(sh)`; `w=np.exp(sh - logs_shift)`.
       grad_a=...
       gy=grad_a - a*grad_a.sum()
       return val, gy
    bounds=[(-50,50)]*n
    res=minimize(fgy, y0, method='L-BFGS-B', jac=True, bounds=bounds, options={'maxiter':1000, 'ftol':1e-14, 'gtol':1e-8, 'maxfun':10000})
    if res.x success:
      a=softmax...
      sc=eval(a); if sc<best...
    return
```

Implement `_logsumexp_shift(x)`:
```
def _logsumexp_shift(x):
    m = float(x.max())
    if m < -745.0:
        return m
    e = np.exp(x - m)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not math.isfinite(s):
        return m
    return m + math.log(s)
```
Since x maybe array length 9611, fine.
For current weights, use shift and logsum; `w = np.exp(shift - logsum)`; sum maybe 1. If logsum from _logsum. If some shift underflow, okay.

In candidate F, we don't need weights, just logs. Use same function.

Now, code details for `_block_expand(a, B)`:
```
def _block_expand(a, B):
   n=a.size; out=np.zeros(n)
   for b in range(B):
       l=b*n//B; r=(b+1)*n//B
       m=a[l:r].mean() if r>l else 0.0
       out[l:r]=m
   s=out.sum()
   if s <= 1e-15: return a.copy()
   return out/s
```
Using Python loop B up to 128 okay.

Now, L-BFGS objective gradient if `tau` based on M; if M changes, τ changes, objective not globally smooth across evaluations. L-BFGS assumes smooth; but between evaluations, tau changes with a, causing objective non-smooth (because τ=M*frac depends on a). This can hurt. def _conv_b(a):
   Fa=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
   b=np.fft.irfft(Fa*Fa,N)[:L]
   np.maximum(b,0.0,out=b)
   return b, Fa
```

def _project_simplex(v):
   v=np.where(np.isfinite(v), v, 0.0)
   if n==1: return np.array([1.0])
   u=np.sort(v)[::-1]
   cssum=np.cumsum(u)
   rho=np.arange(1,n+1)
   cond=u > (cssum-1.0)/rho
   idx=int(np.cumsum(cond[-1]? ) )
```

from scipy.optimize import minimize as _scipy_minimize  # optional maybe inside

def _next_pow2(x):
   p=1
   while p < x: p <<=1
   return max(p,16)

def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):

Let's write `_optimize` code specifically:
```
def _optimize(a0, deadline, rng_local):
    a = _normalize(a0)
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_score = _exact(a)
    if not math.isfinite(best_score):
        best_a = np.ones(n)/n; best_score = 2.0
    N_local=N? Use outer N.
    tau_frac=0.12
    t=0.7
    stall=0
    exact_stall=0
    kick_count=0
    last_exact=time.monotonic()
    it=0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 5.0:
       it +=1
       try:
          b, Fa = _conv_b(a)
          M = float(b.max())
          if not math.isfinite(M) or M <=0: break
          tau = max(1e-9, tau_frac * M)  # 1e-9 maybe; if M~1e-4, okay. Use 1e-10.
          shift = (b - M) / tau
          logsum = _logsumexp_shift(shift)
          Fcur = M + tau * logsum
          w = np.exp(np.clip(shift - logsum, -745, 700))
          ws = w.sum()
          if ws <=0: w = np.ones(L)/L
          else: w = w/ws
          grad = _grad_from_weights(w, Fa)
          gstep = grad - (grad.sum()/n)
          ginf = float(np.max(np.abs(gstep)))
          if not math.isfinite(ginf) or ginf < 1e-14:
              tau_frac = min(tau_frac * 1.5, 0.35)
              stall += 10
              continue
          success=False
          best_cand=None; best_F=Fcur; best_M=M
          tt=t
          for _ in range(8):
              P = _project_simplex(a - tt * gstep)
              bP, _ = _conv_b(P)
              MP = float(bP.max())
              if not math.isfinite(MP): break
              shP = (bP - M) / tau
              logP = _logsumexp_shift(shP)
              Fp = M + tau * logP
              if math.isfinite(Fp) and Fp < best_F:
                  best_F=Fp; best_M=MP; best_cand=P
              if math.isfinite(Fp) and (Fp <= Fcur - 1e-12 * max(1.0, abs(Fcur)) or MP < M - 1e-13):
                  a = P
                  t = min(tt * 1.05, 50.0)
                  success=True
                  stall=0
                  break
              tt *= 0.5
          if not success:
              if best_cand is not None and best_cand is not a? and best_F <= Fcur + 1e-12:
                  a = best_cand
                  t = max(t*0.7, 1e-3)
              else:
                  t = max(t*0.5, 1e-4)
              stall += 1
          score_approx = 2.0 * n * M
          if it % 80 == 0 or (math.isfinite(score_approx) and score_approx < best_score * 0.9995 and time.monotonic() - last_exact > 0.5):
              sc = _exact(a)
              if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                  best_score=sc; best_a=a.copy(); last_exact=time.monotonic(); exact_stall=0
              else:
                  exact_stall += 1
              last_exact=time.monotonic()
          if stall > 60:
              if tau_frac > 0.02:
                  tau_frac *= 0.8
              else:
                  t = min(t*1.5, 50.0)
              stall=0
          if stall > 200 or (exact_stall > 4 and kick_count < 8 and it % 200 == 0):
              # kick
              v = a + rng_local.normal(0.0, 0.25 / max(1,n), n)
              a = _project_simplex(np.maximum(v, 0.0))
              t=0.7; tau_frac=0.12; stall=0; exact_stall=0; kick_count += 1
          if time.monotonic() > deadline - 25: break
       except Exception:
          # restore a from best if needed and break?
          # try normalize best? We can set a=best_a and break to avoid long loop
          a = _normalize(best_a)
          break
    sc=_exact(a)
    if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
       best_score=sc; best_a=a.copy()
    scb=_exact(best_a)
    if not math.isfinite(scb): best_score=2.0; best_a=np.ones(n)/n
    return best_a, best_score

def _coord_polish(a0, deadline, rng_local):
    a = _normalize(a0); best_a=a.copy(); best_score=_exact(a)
    if not finite: return uniform, inf
    accepted=0; last_exact=time.monotonic(); it=0

       it+=1

          eidx = 2*i

def _lbfgs_polish(a0, deadline, tau_frac=0.04):
    if not _scipy_minimize: return a0,inf
    a = _normalize(a0)
    best_a=a.copy(); best_score=_exact(a)

       except Exception: pass
    # run1
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 30: run(a, min(deadline - 30, time+...), tau_frac)
    # run2 maybe smaller tau if time >60

```python
import time
import math
import numpy as np


def _next_pow2(x: int) -> int:
    p = 16
    while p < int(max(16, x)):
        p <<= 1
    return p


def propose_candidate(seed: int = 42, budget_s: float = 1300.0, **kwargs):
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    budget = max(20.0, float(budget_s))
    hard_deadline = t0 + max(5.0, budget - 20.0)

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Get a valid base length/sequence.  height_sequence_1 is provided by
    # the harness when available; otherwise use a moderate default length.
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    try:
        base_arr = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    except Exception:
        base_arr = np.ones(4806, dtype=np.float64)

    if base_arr.size == 0 or not np.all(np.isfinite(base_arr)):
        base_arr = np.ones(4806, dtype=np.float64)

    n = int(base_arr.size)
    if n < 1:
        n = 4806
        base_arr = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)

    L = 2 * n - 1
    N = _next_pow2(3 * n - 2)

    def _normalize(x):
        x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if x.size != n:
            if x.size > n:
                x = x[:n]
            else:
                y = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
                m = min(n, x.size)
                if m > 0:
                    y[:m] = x[:m]
                x = y
        x = np.where(np.isfinite(x), x, 0.0)
        x = np.clip(x, 0.0, 1000.0)
        s = float(x.sum())
        if not math.isfinite(s) or s < 1e-15:
            return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
        return x / s

    def _exact(a):
        try:
            lst = [float(v) for v in np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()]
            sc = float(evaluate_sequence(lst))
            if math.isfinite(sc):
                return sc
        except Exception:
            pass
        return float("inf")

    def _conv_b(a):
        Fa = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
        b = np.fft.irfft(Fa * Fa, N)[:L]
        np.maximum(b, 0.0, out=b)
        return b, Fa

    def _logsumexp_shift(x):
        x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)
        m = float(x.max())
        if not math.isfinite(m):
            return -1e300
        if m < -745.0:
            return m
        e = np.exp(x - m)
        s = float(e.sum())
        if s <= 0.0 or not math.isfinite(s):
            return m
        return m + math.log(s)

    def _grad_from_weights(w, Fa):
        wr = w[::-1]
        Fw = np.fft.rfft(wr, N)
        T = np.fft.irfft(Fa * Fw, N)
        S = T[n - 1:2 * n - 1][::-1]
        np.maximum(S, 0.0, out=S)
        return 2.0 * S

    def _project_simplex(v):
        v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if v.size != n:
            v = _normalize(v)
        v = np.where(np.isfinite(v), v, 0.0)
        if n == 1:
            return np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64)
        u = np.sort(v)[::-1]
        cssum = np.cumsum(u)
        rho = np.arange(1, n + 1, dtype=np.float64)
        cond = u > (cssum - 1.0) / rho
        cnt = int(np.count_nonzero(cond))
        if cnt <= 0:
            cnt = 1
        theta = (cssum[cnt - 1] - 1.0) / float(cnt)
        out = np.maximum(v - theta, 0.0)
        s = float(out.sum())
        if s > 1e-15:
            out /= s
        else:
            out = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
        return out

    def _block_expand(a, B):
        a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        B = int(max(1, min(B, n)))
        out = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        for k in range(B):
            l = k * n // B
            r = (k + 1) * n // B
            if r <= l:
                continue
            m = float(a[l:r].mean())
            out[l:r] = m
        s = float(out.sum())
        if s <= 1e-15:
            return _normalize(a)
        return out / s

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Initial feasible best.
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    base = _normalize(base_arr)
    best_a = base.copy()
    best_score = _exact(best_a)
    if not math.isfinite(best_score):
        best_a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
        best_score = 2.0

    usable = max(10.0, budget - 30.0)
    reserve = min(420.0, max(50.0, 0.28 * usable))
    phase1_deadline = min(hard_deadline - 30.0, t0 + max(20.0, usable - reserve))

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Bounded deterministic start search: try cheap perturbations/shifts
    # and keep the best exact-score candidate as the optimization start.
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def _choose_start(base_a, deadline):
        best_local_a = base_a.copy()
        best_local_s = _exact(best_local_a)

        cands = [base_a]

        x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
        xm = x - 0.5

        for gamma in (0.10, 0.22, 0.35):
            for sgn in (-1.0, 1.0):
                v = np.maximum(base_a * (1.0 + sgn * gamma * xm), 0.0)
                cands.append(_normalize(v))

        for k in (n // 5, -n // 5, n // 4, -n // 4, n // 3, -n // 3):
            cands.append(_normalize(np.roll(base_a, k)))

        for lam in (1.0, 1.5, 2.2):
            v = np.exp(lam * xm)
            cands.append(_normalize(v))
            v = np.exp(-lam * xm)
            cands.append(_normalize(v))

        cands.append(_block_expand(base_a, 64))
        cands.append(_block_expand(base_a, 128))

        for v in cands:
            if time.monotonic() > deadline:
                break
            try:
                v = _normalize(v)
                sc = _exact(v)
            except Exception:
                continue
            if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_local_s:
                best_local_s = sc
                best_local_a = v.copy()

        return best_local_a, best_local_s

    explor_deadline = min(phase1_deadline - 15.0, t0 + min(45.0, 0.12 * usable))
    if explor_deadline > t0 + 8.0 and best_score > 1.50274349:
        try:
            start_a, start_s = _choose_start(best_a, explor_deadline)
            if math.isfinite(start_s) and start_s < best_score:
                best_score = start_s
                best_a = start_a
        except Exception:
            start_a = best_a
    else:
        start_a = best_a

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Main projected-gradient polish on the probability simplex.
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def _optimize(a0, deadline, rng_local):
        a = _normalize(a0)
        local_best_a = a.copy()
        local_best_s = _exact(a)
        if not math.isfinite(local_best_s):
            local_best_a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
            local_best_s = 2.0

        tau_frac = 0.12
        step = 0.7
        stall = 0
        exact_stall = 0
        kick_count = 0
        last_exact = time.monotonic()

        while time.monotonic() < deadline - 5.0:
            try:
                b, Fa = _conv_b(a)
                M = float(b.max())
                if not math.isfinite(M) or M <= 0.0:
                    break

                tau = max(1e-10, tau_frac * M)
                shift = (b - M) / tau
                logsum = _logsumexp_shift(shift)
                Fcur = M + tau * logsum

                w = np.exp(np.clip(shift - logsum, -745.0, 700.0))
                ws = float(w.sum())
                if ws <= 0.0 or not math.isfinite(ws):
                    w = np.ones(L, dtype=np.float64) / float(L)
                else:
                    w /= ws

                grad = _grad_from_weights(w, Fa)
                g = grad - (float(grad.sum()) / float(n))
                gmax = float(np.max(np.abs(g)))
                if not math.isfinite(gmax) or gmax < 1e-14:
                    tau_frac = min(tau_frac * 1.5, 0.35)
                    stall += 10
                    continue

                success = False
                best_cand = None
                best_F = Fcur
                best_M = M
                tt = step

                for _ in range(8):
                    P = _project_simplex(a - tt * g)
                    bP, _ = _conv_b(P)
                    MP = float(bP.max())
                    if not math.isfinite(MP):
                        break

                    shP = (bP - M) / tau
                    logP = _logsumexp_shift(shP)
                    FP = M + tau * logP

                    if math.isfinite(FP) and FP < best_F:
                        best_F = FP
                        best_M = MP
                        best_cand = P

                    if math.isfinite(FP) and (
                        FP <= Fcur - 1e-12 * max(1.0, abs(Fcur))
                        or MP < M - 1e-13
                    ):
                        a = P
                        step = min(tt * 1.05, 50.0)
                        success = True
                        stall = 0
                        break

                    tt *= 0.5

                if success:
                    approxM = best_M
                else:
                    if best_cand is not None and best_F <= Fcur + 1e-12:
                        a = best_cand
                        step = max(step * 0.7, 1e-3)
                        approxM = best_M
                    else:
                        step = max(step * 0.5, 1e-4)
                        approxM = M

                score_approx = 2.0 * float(n) * approxM
                if (
                    (it_local := 0) >= 0  # no-op to keep Python happy in older versions
                ):
                    pass

                # Exact re-check, rate-limited.
                if (
                    (time.monotonic() - last_exact) > 0.7
                    and math.isfinite(score_approx)
                ):
                    if math.isfinite(score_approx) and score_approx < local_best_s * 0.9995:
                        sc = _exact(a)
                        if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < local_best_s:
                            local_best_s = sc
                            local_best_a = a.copy()
                        else:
                            exact_stall += 1
                        last_exact = time.monotonic()

                if stall > 60:
                    if tau_frac > 0.02:
                        tau_frac *= 0.8
                    else:
                        step = min(step * 1.5, 50.0)
                    stall = 0

                if (stall > 220) or (exact_stall > 5 and kick_count < 8):
                    v = a + rng_local.normal(0.0, 0.30 / float(n), n)
                    a = _project_simplex(np.maximum(v, 0.0))
                    step = 0.7
                    tau_frac = 0.12
                    stall = 0
                    exact_stall = 0
                    kick_count += 1

                if time.monotonic() > deadline - 25.0:
                    break

            except Exception:
                a = _normalize(local_best_a)
                break

        sc = _exact(a)
        if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < local_best_s:
            local_best_s = sc
            local_best_a = a.copy()

        scb = _exact(local_best_a)
        if not math.isfinite(scb):
            local_best_a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
            local_best_s = 2.0

        return local_best_a, local_best_s

    if phase1_deadline > time.monotonic() + 15.0:
        try:
            a1, s1 = _optimize(start_a, phase1_deadline, rng)
            if math.isfinite(s1) and s1 < best_score:
                best_score = s1
                best_a = a1
        except Exception:
            pass

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Optional full-dimensional L-BFGS polish in log/softmax coordinates.
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def _lbfgs_polish(a0, deadline):
        try:
            from scipy.optimize import minimize as _minimize
        except Exception:
            return None, float("inf")

        local_best_a = _normalize(a0)
        local_best_s = _exact(local_best_a)
        if not math.isfinite(local_best_s):
            return None, float("inf")

        for tau_frac_run in (0.055, 0.022):
            if time.monotonic() > deadline - 70.0:
                break

            try:
                b0, _ = _conv_b(local_best_a)
                M0 = float(b0.max())
                if not math.isfinite(M0) or M0 <= 0.0:
                    break
                tau_run = max(1e-10, tau_frac_run * M0)

                y0 = np.log(np.maximum(local_best_a, 1e-300))
                y0 -= float(y0.mean())

                class _Abort(Exception):
                    pass

                deadline_run = min(deadline - 30.0, time.monotonic() + max(60.0, 0.35 * reserve))

                def fun(y):
                    if time.monotonic() > deadline_run:
                        raise _Abort
                    y = np.asarray(y, dtype=np.float64)
                    y = np.where(np.isfinite(y), y, 0.0)
                    y = np.clip(y, -50.0, 50.0)
                    e = np.exp(y - float(y.max()))
                    a = e / float(e.sum())

                    b, Fa = _conv_b(a)
                    M = float(b.max())
                    if not math.isfinite(M) or M <= 0.0:
                        return 1e100, np.zeros_like(y)

                    shift = (b - M) / tau_run
                    logsum = _logsumexp_shift(shift)
                    val = 2.0 * float(n) * (M + tau_run * logsum)

                    w = np.exp(np.clip(shift - logsum, -745.0, 700.0))
                    ws = float(w.sum())
                    if ws <= 0.0 or not math.isfinite(ws):
                        w = np.ones(L, dtype=np.float64) / float(L)
                    else:
                        w /= ws

                    ga = _grad_from_weights(w, Fa)
                    gy = ga - a * float(ga.sum())
                    gy = np.where(np.isfinite(gy), gy, 0.0)
                    return float(val), gy

                bounds = [(-50.0, 50.0)] * n
                res = _minimize(
                    fun,
                    y0.copy(),
                    method="L-BFGS-B",
                    jac=True,
                    bounds=bounds,
                    options={
                        "maxiter": 500,
                        "maxfun": 7000,
                        "ftol": 1e-14,
                        "gtol": 1e-8,
                    },
                )
                if hasattr(res, "x"):
                    y = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
                    y = np.clip(y, -50.0, 50.0)
                    e = np.exp(y - float(y.max()))
                    a = e / float(e.sum())
                    sc = _exact(a)
                    if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < local_best_s:
                        local_best_s = sc
                        local_best_a = a.copy()

            except Exception:
                break

        scb = _exact(local_best_a)
        if not math.isfinite(scb):
            return None, float("inf")
        return local_best_a, local_best_s

    if time.monotonic() < hard_deadline - 160.0:
        try:
            lb_dead = min(hard_deadline - 80.0, time.monotonic() + max(80.0, 0.35 * reserve))
            a2, s2 = _lbfgs_polish(best_a, lb_dead)
            if a2 is not None and math.isfinite(s2) and s2 < best_score:
                best_score = s2
                best_a = a2
        except Exception:
            pass

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Coordinate polish: direct one-coordinate searches on the exact
    # (FFT-approximated) quadratic changes.
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def _coord_polish(a0, deadline, rng_local):
        a = _normalize(a0)
        local_best_a = a.copy()
        local_best_s = _exact(a)
        if not math.isfinite(local_best_s):
            return local_best_a, 2.0

        accepted = 0
        last_exact = time.monotonic()
        it = 0

        while time.monotonic() < deadline - 8.0:
            it += 1
            try:
                i = int(rng_local.integers(0, n))
                old = float(a[i])

                b, _ = _conv_b(a)
                M0 = float(b.max())
                if not math.isfinite(M0) or M0 <= 0.0:
                    break

                j = np.arange(L, dtype=np.int64)
                off = j - i
                mask = (off >= 0) & (off < n) & (off != 0)
                d = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
                d[mask] = 2.0 * a[off[mask]]
                eidx = 2 * i

                mean = float(a.mean())
                if old > 1e-15:
                    cands = [old * f for f in (0.25, 0.5, 0.75, 1.25, 1.5, 2.0)]
                else:
                    cands = [0.0, 0.05 * mean, 0.2 * mean, mean, 5.0 * mean, 20.0 * mean]
                cands.append(old)

                best_x = old
                best_sc = 2.0 * float(n) * M0

                for xv in cands:
                    xv = float(xv)
                    if not math.isfinite(xv) or xv < 0.0:
                        continue
                    S = 1.0 + (xv - old)
                    if S <= 1e-12:
                        continue
                    if abs(xv - old) <= 1e-16:
                        sc = best_sc
                    else:
                        tmp = b + d * (xv - old)
                        if 0 <= eidx < L:
                            tmp = tmp.copy()
                            tmp[eidx] += xv * xv - old * old
                        m = float(tmp.max())
                        if m < 0.0:
                            m = 0.0
                        sc = 2.0 * float(n) * m / (S * S)
                    if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc - 1e-13:
                        best_sc = sc
                        best_x = xv

                if best_x != old:
                    a[i] = best_x
                    s = float(a.sum())
                    if s > 1e-15:
                        a /= s
                    else:
                        a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
                    accepted += 1

                    if (
                        accepted % 25 == 0
                        or (
                            math.isfinite(best_sc)
                            and best_sc < local_best_s * 0.999
                            and (time.monotonic() - last_exact) > 0.6
                        )
                    ):
                        sc = _exact(a)
                        if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < local_best_s:
                            local_best_s = sc
                            local_best_a = a.copy()
                        last_exact = time.monotonic()

                elif it % 400 == 0 and (time.monotonic() - last_exact) > 2.0:
                    sc = _exact(a)
                    if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < local_best_s:
                        local_best_s = sc
                        local_best_a = a.copy()
                    last_exact = time.monotonic()

            except Exception:
                break

        sc = _exact(a)
        if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < local_best_s:
            local_best_s = sc
            local_best_a = a.copy()

        scb = _exact(local_best_a)
        if not math.isfinite(scb):
            local_best_a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
            local_best_s = 2.0

        return local_best_a, local_best_s

    if time.monotonic() < hard_deadline - 50.0:
        try:
            co_dead = min(hard_deadline - 15.0, time.monotonic() + max(60.0, 0.45 * reserve))
            a3, s3 = _coord_polish(best_a, co_dead, rng)
            if math.isfinite(s3) and s3 < best_score:
                best_score = s3
                best_a = a3
        except Exception:
            pass

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Final safety validation.
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    final_a = _normalize(best_a)
    final_sc = _exact(final_a)
    if not math.isfinite(final_sc):
        final_a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    else:
        # If the stored best score is better than the normalized candidate due to scaling
        # (should be invariant but guard against numerical aliasing), keep final_a anyway.
        # The objective is scale invariant, so normalization does not hurt.
        pass

    return [float(v) for v in final_a]
```
